  • 术前测试中,干预组有40.2%的学生知识水平较低(M ± SD = 1.68 ± 0.616);术后测试中,55.9%的学生知识水平较高(M ± SD = 1.73 ± 0.448)。在态度方面,干预后73.5%的参与者报告了积极的态度,而26.5%报告了消极的态度,这比术前测试时的60.8%消极态度有所下降。

  • Here, we identified four antibodies that broadly recognize influenza B virus hemagglutinin and protect through distinct mechanisms. [10.1128/jvi.00802-26].
  • The pseudovirus assays showed that subclade K‐HA caused augmented entry into certain cell lines and significant antibody evasion, both of which were attributed to the A186D mutation (A4), and influenza vaccination significantly boosted neutralisation of subclade K, indicating potential vaccine protection (A5).

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Journal of Virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-04  |  DOI: 10.1128/jvi.00802-26  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, e0080226

针对流感B病毒血凝素的广谱反应性抗体通过不同的结构机制中和并提供保护。

Broadly reactive antibodies against influenza B virus hemagglutinin neutralize and protect through distinct structural mechanisms.
作者： Disha Bhavsar, Alesandro Civljak, Bruno Bonnettaz, Guha Asthagiri Arunkumar, Florian Krammer, Goran Bajic
摘要中文翻译摘要 流感B病毒对季节性疾病负担有显著贡献;然而,抗体如何识别主要糖蛋白血凝素(HA)并介导抗病毒药物活性的结构基础仍不完全明确。流感B病毒过去曾分为两个抗原不同的谱系,即B/Victoria/2/1987样和B/Yamagata/16/1988样,但后者近年来在全球监测中未被检测到。HA中的抗原漂移会导致疫苗效果减弱;然而,抗体如何识别流感B病毒HA并介导抗病毒药物活性的结构和功能基础仍不完全明确,从而阻碍了我们指导下一代疫苗设计以实现广泛保护的努力。我们表征了四种广谱结合并中和流感B病毒的鼠单克隆抗体(mAb),这些抗体能够中和跨越四个十年的抗原漂移分离株。通过结合冷冻电镜与体外和体内功能实验,我们发现这些抗体靶向HA的不同区域,并通过多种机制赋予抗病毒药物活性。一种抗体结合受体结合位点并强力抑制血凝,而其他抗体则干扰病毒释放并抑制神经氨酸酶(NA)活性,表明NA被空间阻断。一种中段连接区抗体在体外还诱导抗体依赖性细胞毒性。尽管这些机制存在差异,当这些抗体在预防性或治疗性给药时,均能完全保护小鼠。综上所述,这些发现定义了抗体对流感B病毒HA的不同识别模式,并将表位特异性与抗病毒药物功能联系起来,提供了免疫保护相关性的机制理解,并为诱导针对流感B病毒的广谱保护性抗体反应提供了指导。重要性 流感B病毒导致显著的季节性疾病,尤其是在儿童中;然而,针对流感B病毒的抗体反应仍不如针对流感A病毒的抗体反应那样被充分理解。在这里,我们鉴定了四种能够广谱识别流感B病毒血凝素并通过不同机制提供保护的抗体。我们确定了三种抗体-血凝素复合物的冷冻电镜结构,以定义其表位并解释其分子作用机制。一种抗体通过结合受体结合位点阻断病毒附着,而靶向中段连接区的抗体则通过进入后的抗病毒药物活性、神经氨酸酶抑制或免疫效应功能起作用。尽管这些抗体在中和效力上存在差异,但当在感染前给药时,均能保护小鼠,且其中一些在感染后仍保持有效性。这些发现表明,针对流感B病毒的广谱保护可以通过多种抗体靶点和机制实现,为未来疫苗和抗体疗法的评估与设计提供了指导。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
重要性 流感B病毒导致显著的季节性疾病,尤其是在儿童中;然而,针对流感B病毒的抗体反应仍不如针对流感A病毒的抗体反应那样被充分理解。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
使用冷冻电子显微镜结合体外和体内功能测定,我们表明这些抗体靶向 HA 的不同区域并通过多种机制赋予 抗病毒药物 活性。
主要结果
在这里,我们鉴定了四种广泛识别乙型流感病毒血凝素并通过不同机制提供保护的抗体。
解释与创新
我们确定了三种抗体-血凝素复合物的冷冻电子显微镜结构,以定义它们的表位并解释它们的分子作用机制。
科研与公卫意义
B型流感病毒过去以两种抗原性不同的谱系传播,B/Victoria/2/1987样和B/Yamagata/16/1988样,尽管近年来在全球监测中尚未检测到后者。
局限与证据强度
尽管这些抗体的中和效力不同,但所有在感染前施用的小鼠都受到保护,并且有几种在感染后仍然有效。

Broadly reactive antibodies against influenza B virus hemagglutinin neutralize and protect through distinct structural mechanisms.

Authors: Disha Bhavsar, Alesandro Civljak, Bruno Bonnettaz, Guha Asthagiri Arunkumar, Florian Krammer, Goran Bajic
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Influenza B viruses contribute substantially to seasonal disease burden; however, the structural basis by which antibodies recognize the major glycoprotein hemagglutinin (HA) and mediate antiviral activity remains incompletely defined. Influenza B virus used to circulate as two antigenically distinct lineages, B/Victoria/2/1987-like and B/Yamagata/16/1988-like, although the latter has not been detected in global surveillance in recent years. Antigenic drift in HA contributes to reduced vaccine effectiveness; however, the structural and functional basis by which antibodies recognize influenza B virus HA and mediate antiviral activity remains incompletely defined and thus thwarts our efforts in guiding next-generation vaccine design for broad protection. We characterize four murine monoclonal antibodies (mAb) that broadly bind and neutralize influenza B viruses spanning isolates across four decades of antigenic drift. Using cryo-electron microscopy coupled with in vitro and in vivo functional assays, we show that these antibodies target distinct regions of HA and confer antiviral activity through multiple mechanisms. One antibody engages the receptor-binding site and potently inhibits hemagglutination, whereas others interfere with viral egress and inhibit neuraminidase (NA) activity, suggesting steric occlusion of NA. A medial-junction antibody additionally induces antibody-dependent cellular cytotoxicity in vitro . Despite these mechanistic differences, all antibodies confer complete protection in mice when administered prophylactically or therapeutically. Together, these findings define distinct modes of antibody recognition of influenza B virus HA and link epitope specificity to antiviral function, providing a mechanistic understanding of correlates of immune protection and informing efforts to elicit broadly protective antibody responses against influenza B viruses. IMPORTANCE Influenza B viruses cause substantial seasonal illness, particularly in children; however, antibody responses against influenza B virus remain less well understood than those against influenza A virus. Here, we identified four antibodies that broadly recognize influenza B virus hemagglutinin and protect through distinct mechanisms. We determined cryo-electron microscopy structures of three antibody-hemagglutinin complexes to define their epitopes and explain their molecular mechanisms of action. One antibody blocks viral attachment by engaging the receptor-binding site, whereas antibodies targeting the medial junction act through post-entry antiviral activity, neuraminidase inhibition, or immune effector functions. Although the antibodies differed in neutralizing potency, all protected mice when administered before infection, and several remained effective after infection. These findings show that broad protection against influenza B virus can arise through multiple antibody targets and mechanisms, informing the evaluation and design of future vaccines and antibody-based therapies.

IMPORTANCE Influenza B viruses cause substantial seasonal illness, particularly in children; however, antibody responses against influenza B virus remain less well understood than those against influenza A virus.

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
Influenza B virus used to circulate as two antigenically distinct lineages, B/Victoria/2/1987-like and B/Yamagata/16/1988-like, although the latter has not been detected in global surveillance in recent years.
Limitations and evidence strength
Although the antibodies differed in neutralizing potency, all protected mice when administered before infection, and several remained effective after infection.

宿主 H3N2甲型流感病毒K亚群的细胞进入和中和敏感性。

Host cell entry and neutralisation sensitivity of H3N2 influenza A virus subclade K.
作者： Nicole de Moor, Nianzhen Chen, Georg M. N. Behrens, Lu Zhang, Anna-Sophie Moldenhauer, Michael Winkler, Christine Happle, Sandra Steffens, Metodi V. Stankov, Karsten Lürken
摘要中文翻译2025年,出现了突变的H3N2谱系,K亚支,在许多地区表现出高活性。血凝素 (HA) 中的 突变 可能会影响细胞进入和抗体介导的中和。使用假病毒颗粒,我们发现 K-HA 亚支可促进进入某些细胞系并表现出显着的抗体逃避。两种表型均与 突变 A186D 相关。流感 疫苗接种 显着增强了 H3N2 亚支 K 的中和作用,表明当前的 疫苗 可能提供相当大的保护。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2025 年 H3N2 亚支 K 突变的出现引起了人们对其区域活性增强的担忧,目前尚不清楚血凝素 突变 如何改变病毒进入和抗体介导的中和作用,促使研究人员对这些特性进行调查。
设计与对象
该研究是使用假病毒颗粒测定来评估 K-HA 分支的进入和中和,但提供的证据没有具体说明研究日期、中心、样本量或参与者组成。
核心方法
研究人员使用假病毒颗粒量化了 K-HA 分支 (A3) 的进入和中和。
主要结果
假病毒检测显示,K-HA 亚型导致进入某些细胞系的增加和显着的抗体逃避,这两者均归因于 A186D 突变 (A4),而流感 疫苗接种 显着增强 K 亚型的中和,表明 疫苗 具有潜在的保护作用 (A5)。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,尽管进入和抗体逃避增加,但当前流感 疫苗 仍保持针对 K 亚支的强中和作用,这表明现有的 疫苗接种 策略仍然具有保护作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果意味着现有的 H3N2 疫苗 可能对 K 分支提供相当大的保护,支持持续的 疫苗接种 计划,并强调对影响进入和中和的 HA 监测 的 监测 的需要。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告任何设计限制、偏差、样本量限制或普遍性问题,并且进入和中和效应的直接实验证明产生了中等的证据强度。

Host cell entry and neutralisation sensitivity of H3N2 influenza A virus subclade K.

Authors: Nicole de Moor, Nianzhen Chen, Georg M. N. Behrens, Lu Zhang, Anna-Sophie Moldenhauer, Michael Winkler, Christine Happle, Sandra Steffens, Metodi V. Stankov, Karsten Lürken
Original abstract (English)
In 2025, a mutated H3N2 lineage, subclade K, emerged, showing high activity in many regions. Mutations in the hemagglutinin (HA) may affect cell entry and antibody-mediated neutralisation. Using pseudovirus particles, we show that subclade K-HA drives augmented entry into certain cell lines and displays significant antibody evasion. Both phenotypes were linked to mutation A186D. Influenza vaccination significantly boosted H3N2 subclade K neutralisation, suggesting that current vaccines may provide considerable protection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The emergence of a mutated H3N2 subclade K in 2025 raised concerns about its heightened regional activity, and it was unclear how hemagglutinin mutations might alter viral entry and antibody‐mediated neutralisation, prompting the study to investigate these properties.
Design and population
The study was performed using pseudovirus particle assays to evaluate entry and neutralisation of subclade K‐HA, but the supplied evidence does not specify the study dates, centres, sample size, or participant composition.
Core methods
Using pseudovirus particles, the investigators quantified entry and neutralisation of subclade K‐HA (A3).
Main results
The pseudovirus assays showed that subclade K‐HA caused augmented entry into certain cell lines and significant antibody evasion, both of which were attributed to the A186D mutation (A4), and influenza vaccination significantly boosted neutralisation of subclade K, indicating potential vaccine protection (A5).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors conclude that despite increased entry and antibody evasion, current influenza vaccines maintain strong neutralisation against subclade K, suggesting that existing vaccination strategies remain protective.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results imply that existing H3N2 vaccines are likely to provide considerable protection against subclade K, supporting continued vaccination programs and highlighting the need for surveillance of HA mutations that affect entry and neutralisation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report any design limitations, bias, sample size constraints, or generalisability issues, and the direct experimental demonstration of entry and neutralisation effects yields a moderate evidence strength.

与 季节性流感 相比,不同 COVID-19 大流行期间危重疾病的预测因素

Predictors of Critical Illness During Different COVID-19 Pandemic Periods Compared With Seasonal Influenza
作者： Josefine Beck-Friis, Ailiana Santosa, Huiqi Li, Fredrik Nyberg, Magnus Gisslén, Jonatan Oras, Aylin Yilmaz
摘要中文翻译目标:自 COVID-19 大流行爆发以来,不断出现的病毒变种、人群免疫力的增强以及不断发展的 治疗 策略已大大改变了疾病的严重程度。本研究旨在确定一段时间内因 COVID-19 导致的危重疾病的预测因素,并将其与瑞典的 季节性流感 的预测因素进行比较。设计、设置、患者和测量:我们利用全国注册数据进行了一项回顾性、基于人群的 队列研究 研究。根据大流行浪潮和主要病毒变种,2020 年至 2022 年间所有因 COVID-19 住院的个人被分为五组。第六组包括 2018 年至 2022 年间因流感住院的所有患者。对患者从住院到入住 ICU、院内死亡、出院或研究结束进行随访。计算了 发病率 率,Cox 回归确定了 ICU 入院和住院 发病率 的预测因子,调整了年龄、性别、社会经济因素、合并症和严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2-疫苗接种。登记册中未提供有关流感 疫苗接种 的数据。主要结果:我们纳入了 92,428 例 COVID-19 住院患者和 21,355 例流感住院患者。Delta 主导时期的住院率最低,而 Omicron 主导时期的 ICU 入院率最低。Omicron 时期与流感住院患者人口统计、住院时间、ICU 入院情况和住院 死亡率 最为相似。不同大流行时期的年龄分布各不相同,Omicron 时期的年龄分布与流感相似(约 50% ≥ 75 岁)。在未接种疫苗的患者、男性和 45-74 岁的 COVID-19 患者中,以及在 45-64 岁或患有流感慢性肺部疾病的个体中,入住 ICU 的调整后风险比最强。院内 死亡率 的常见预测因素包括年龄超过 45 岁(74 岁以后急剧增加)、男性和慢性合并症,以及 COVID-19 的未接种疫苗状态。结论:尽管 COVID-19 的住院率始终高于流感,但在大流行后期阶段,人口统计和临床模式趋于一致。在这两种感染中,高龄是严重疾病的最强预测因素,缺乏 疫苗接种 还与严重的 COVID-19 相关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
自 COVID-19 大流行爆发以来,不断出现的病毒变种、人群免疫力的增强以及不断发展的 治疗 策略已大大改变了疾病的严重程度。本研究旨在确定一段时间内因 COVID-19 导致的危重疾病的预测因素,并将其与瑞典的 季节性流感 的预测因素进行比较。
设计与对象
我们使用全国登记数据进行了一项回顾性、基于人群的 队列研究 研究。根据大流行浪潮和主要病毒变种,2020 年至 2022 年间所有因 COVID-19 住院的个人被分为五组。第六组包括 2018 年至 2022 年期间因流感住院的所有患者。
核心方法
计算了 发病率 率,Cox 回归确定了 ICU 入院和住院 发病率 的预测因子,调整了年龄、性别、社会经济因素、合并症和严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2-疫苗接种。
主要结果
我们纳入了 92,428 例 COVID-19 住院病例和 21,355 例流感住院病例。Delta 主导时期的住院率最低,而 Omicron 主导时期的 ICU 入院率最低。Omicron 时期与流感住院患者人口统计、住院时间、ICU 入院情况和住院 死亡率 最为相似。
解释与创新
尽管 COVID-19 的住院率始终高于流感,但人口和临床模式在大流行后期阶段趋于一致。该研究强调了疾病严重程度随时间的变化以及两种感染之间模式的趋同。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 监测 和 季节性流感 以及 COVID-19 大流行后期阶段的风险评估策略提供信息。他们还提出了 疫苗接种 在减轻这两种感染的严重后果方面的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对登记数据的依赖而受到限制,这些数据可能缺少有关流感 疫苗接种 的信息。证据是中等的,因为研究结果基于观察数据并针对已知的混杂因素进行了调整。

Predictors of Critical Illness During Different COVID-19 Pandemic Periods Compared With Seasonal Influenza

Authors: Josefine Beck-Friis, Ailiana Santosa, Huiqi Li, Fredrik Nyberg, Magnus Gisslén, Jonatan Oras, Aylin Yilmaz
Original abstract (English)
Objectives: Since the onset of the COVID-19-pandemic, emerging virus variants, increasing population immunity, and evolving treatment strategies have substantially altered disease severity. This study aimed to identify predictors of critical illness due to COVID-19 over time and compare them with those of seasonal influenza in Sweden. Design, Setting, Patients, and Measurements: We conducted a retrospective, population-based cohort study using nationwide registry data. All individuals hospitalized for COVID-19 between 2020 and 2022 were categorized into five groups, based on pandemic waves and dominating virus variants. A sixth group included all patients hospitalized for influenza between 2018 and 2022. Patients were followed from hospitalization until ICU admission, in-hospital death, discharge, or end of study. Incidence rates were calculated and Cox regression identified predictors of ICU admission and in-hospital mortality, adjusting for age, sex, socioeconomic factors, comorbidities, and severe acute respiratory syndrome corona virus 2-vaccination. Data on influenza vaccinations were not available from the registers. Main Results: We included 92,428 COVID-19 hospitalizations and 21,355 influenza hospitalizations. Hospitalization rates were lowest during the delta-dominant period, while ICU admission rates were lowest during the Omicron-dominant period. The Omicron period most closely resembled influenza inpatient demographics, hospital stay duration, ICU admissions, and in-hospital mortality. Age distributions varied across pandemic periods, with the Omicron period showing an age profile similar to influenza (≈ 50% ≥ 75 yr). Adjusted hazard ratios for ICU-admission were strongest in unvaccinated patients, males, and individuals of 45–74 years for COVID-19, and in individuals of 45–64 years or with chronic lung diseases for influenza. Common predictors of in-hospital mortality included age older than 45 years (with a sharp increase after 74), male sex, and chronic comorbidities, and for COVID-19 also unvaccinated status. Conclusions: Although COVID-19 consistently resulted in higher hospitalization rates than influenza, demographic and clinical patterns converged during the later pandemic stages. Advanced age was the strongest predictor of severe illness in both infections, with lack of vaccination additionally associated with severe COVID-19.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Since the onset of the COVID-19-pandemic, emerging virus variants, increasing population immunity, and evolving treatment strategies have substantially altered disease severity. This study aimed to identify predictors of critical illness due to COVID-19 over time and compare them with those of seasonal influenza in Sweden.
Design and population
We conducted a retrospective, population-based cohort study using nationwide registry data. All individuals hospitalized for COVID-19 between 2020 and 2022 were categorized into five groups, based on pandemic waves and dominating virus variants. A sixth group included all patients hospitalized for influenza between 2018 and 2022.
Core methods
Incidence rates were calculated and Cox regression identified predictors of ICU admission and in-hospital mortality, adjusting for age, sex, socioeconomic factors, comorbidities, and severe acute respiratory syndrome corona virus 2-vaccination.
Main results
We included 92,428 COVID-19 hospitalizations and 21,355 influenza hospitalizations. Hospitalization rates were lowest during the delta-dominant period, while ICU admission rates were lowest during the Omicron-dominant period. The Omicron period most closely resembled influenza inpatient demographics, hospital stay duration, ICU admissions, and in-hospital mortality.
Interpretation and novelty
Although COVID-19 consistently resulted in higher hospitalization rates than influenza, demographic and clinical patterns converged during the later pandemic stages. The study highlights the changing nature of disease severity over time and the convergence of patterns between the two infections.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and risk assessment strategies for both seasonal influenza and later stages of the COVID-19 pandemic. They also suggest the importance of vaccination in mitigating severe outcomes for both infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on registry data, which may have missing information on influenza vaccinations. The evidence is moderate, as the findings are based on observational data and adjusted for known confounders.

季节性流感 疫苗 与其他 疫苗 联合给药的免疫原性和安全性:系统评价和荟萃分析

Immunogenicity and safety of seasonal influenza vaccine co-administered with other vaccines: a systematic review and meta-analysis
作者： Samuel Asri, Paraskevi C. Fragkou, Elena Ahrens, Svenja S. Hartmann, Clemens Thölken, Chrysanthi Skevaki
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
季节性流感 仍然是全球发病率和 死亡率 的主要原因,凸显了需要提高覆盖范围并简化 疫苗 交付的 疫苗接种 策略。
设计与对象
这项系统评价和荟萃分析包括 52 项合格研究,评估流感 疫苗 与 COVID-19 或其他 疫苗 当天共同给药与非同时给药相比的免疫原性和安全性。比较者包括序贯给药、单次 疫苗 给药或安慰剂对照的延迟 疫苗接种。
核心方法
使用抗体滴度和血清保护率的几何平均倍数上升(GMFR)评估免疫原性。通过不良事件 (AE) 发病率 评估安全性。使用随机试验的 Cochrane 偏倚风险工具和非随机干预研究的偏倚风险评估偏倚风险;证据的确定性通过建议、评估、制定和评估的分级进行评估。
主要结果
Influenza immunogenicity was comparable between the co-administration and non-concomitant comparator group across all strains (H1N1 GMFR ratio of means (ROM): 1.02 [95% CI: 0.95–1.10]; H3N2, 1.05 [95% CI: 0.97–1.13]; B strain, 1.01 [95% CI: 0.97–1.05]). Pooled risk ratio (RR) for seroprotection was 1.00 for all three strains with 95% CIs ranging from 0.99–1.01. GMFR for COVID-19 vaccines was modestly reduced under co-administration (ROM 0.84 [95% CI: 0.74–0.95]; p = 0.006). Serious AEs were more frequent in the co-administration group compared to the non-concomitant group (RR 1.41 [95% CI: 1.07–1.86]; p = 0.014; absolute risk difference: 1.56 percentage points).
解释与创新
总体而言,联合用药保留了流感免疫原性,但适度降低了 COVID-19 疫苗 GMFR。
科研与公卫意义
尽管安全性研究结果需要谨慎解释,但较低的绝对风险差异支持其作为简化 疫苗接种 时间表和提高采用率的策略的可行性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。

Immunogenicity and safety of seasonal influenza vaccine co-administered with other vaccines: a systematic review and meta-analysis

Authors: Samuel Asri, Paraskevi C. Fragkou, Elena Ahrens, Svenja S. Hartmann, Clemens Thölken, Chrysanthi Skevaki
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Seasonal influenza remains a leading cause of global morbidity and mortality, highlighting the need for vaccination strategies that improve coverage and streamline vaccine delivery. In this systematic review and meta-analysis, we searched PubMed, Embase, Web of Science, Scopus, and the Cochrane Central Register of Controlled Trials for randomised controlled trials (RCTs), cohort, case-control, and cross-sectional studies, evaluating immunogenicity and safety of same-day co-administration of influenza vaccines with COVID-19 or other vaccines, compared with non-concomitant administration. Comparators included sequential administration, single vaccine administration or placebo-controlled delayed vaccination. Risk of bias was evaluated using the Cochrane Risk-of-Bias tool for Randomized Trials and Risk of Bias In Non-randomized Studies of Interventions; certainty of evidence was evaluated using Grading of Recommendations, Assessment, Development and Evaluation. Immunogenicity was assessed using geometric mean fold rise (GMFR) in antibody titres and seroprotection rate. Safety was assessed by adverse event (AE) incidence. 52 eligible studies were included. Influenza immunogenicity was comparable between the co-administration and non-concomitant comparator group across all strains (H1N1 GMFR ratio of means (ROM): 1.02 [95% CI: 0.95–1.10]; H3N2, 1.05 [95% CI: 0.97–1.13]; B strain, 1.01 [95% CI: 0.97–1.05]). Pooled risk ratio (RR) for seroprotection was 1.00 for all three strains with 95% CIs ranging from 0.99–1.01. GMFR for COVID-19 vaccines was modestly reduced under co-administration (ROM 0.84 [95% CI: 0.74–0.95]; p = 0.006). Serious AEs were more frequent in the co-administration group compared to the non-concomitant group (RR 1.41 [95% CI: 1.07–1.86]; p = 0.014; absolute risk difference: 1.56 percentage points). Overall, co-administration preserves influenza immunogenicity but modestly reduces COVID-19 vaccine GMFR. Although safety findings warrant cautious interpretation, the low absolute risk difference supports its feasibility as a strategy to streamline vaccination schedules and improve uptake.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Seasonal influenza remains a leading cause of global morbidity and mortality, highlighting the need for vaccination strategies that improve coverage and streamline vaccine delivery.
Design and population
This systematic review and meta-analysis included 52 eligible studies evaluating immunogenicity and safety of same-day co-administration of influenza vaccines with COVID-19 or other vaccines, compared with non-concomitant administration. Comparators included sequential administration, single vaccine administration or placebo-controlled delayed vaccination.
Core methods
Immunogenicity was assessed using geometric mean fold rise (GMFR) in antibody titres and seroprotection rate. Safety was assessed by adverse event (AE) incidence. Risk of bias was evaluated using the Cochrane Risk-of-Bias tool for Randomized Trials and Risk of Bias In Non-randomized Studies of Interventions; certainty of evidence was evaluated using Grading of Recommendations, Assessment, Development and Evaluation.
Main results
Influenza immunogenicity was comparable between the co-administration and non-concomitant comparator group across all strains (H1N1 GMFR ratio of means (ROM): 1.02 [95% CI: 0.95–1.10]; H3N2, 1.05 [95% CI: 0.97–1.13]; B strain, 1.01 [95% CI: 0.97–1.05]). Pooled risk ratio (RR) for seroprotection was 1.00 for all three strains with 95% CIs ranging from 0.99–1.01. GMFR for COVID-19 vaccines was modestly reduced under co-administration (ROM 0.84 [95% CI: 0.74–0.95]; p = 0.006). Serious AEs were more frequent in the co-administration group compared to the non-concomitant group (RR 1.41 [95% CI: 1.07–1.86]; p = 0.014; absolute risk difference: 1.56 percentage points).
Interpretation and novelty
Overall, co-administration preserves influenza immunogenicity but modestly reduces COVID-19 vaccine GMFR.
Scientific and public-health significance
Although safety findings warrant cautious interpretation, the low absolute risk difference supports its feasibility as a strategy to streamline vaccination schedules and improve uptake.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design.

对 季节性流感 疫苗接种 计划的投资为大流行病的防范提供了有力的支持。

Investments in seasonal influenza vaccination programs pack a punch for pandemic preparedness.
作者： Angela K. Shen, Cara Tupps, Silvia Bino, Musole Chipoya, Lia Jabidze, Ani Janashia, Milko Joksimovic, Verica Jovanovic, Nizar Maswadi, Sanjin Musa
摘要中文翻译在 COVID-19 大流行之前,拥有 季节性流感 免疫计划的低收入和中等收入国家能够比没有此类能力的国家更快地提供 疫苗 并实现更高的覆盖率。参与国际 疫苗 倡议伙伴关系 (PIVI) 的中等收入(低收入和高收入)国家描述了流感流程和系统,由于现有能力,这些流程和系统有助于开展更高效、更有效的 COVID-19 大规模 疫苗接种 运动。一些常规系统很容易调整,而另一些则需要进行修改,以满足应对 COVID-19 大流行时对速度和数量的需求。虽然年度流感计划是磨练 疫苗接种 应急响应准备能力的天然“温暖基础”(即,在非危机期间保持人员配置和供应链等运营在最低可持续水平),但仍然需要在更具体的大流行防范领域进行投资。还应探讨合作准备程度和机会的区域差异。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探索对 季节性流感 疫苗接种 项目的投资如何通过利用现有基础设施和能力来加强大流行病的防范。
设计与对象
该研究分析了具有 季节性流感 免疫计划的低收入和中等收入国家的数据,将其 疫苗 免疫计划的交付速度和覆盖范围与在 COVID-19 大流行之前没有此类计划的国家进行了比较。
核心方法
研究人员检查了季节性流感程序的操作流程和系统,确定这些流程和系统如何被调整或用于应对疫情。
主要结果
与没有此类计划的国家相比,实施 季节性流感 免疫计划的低收入和中等收入国家实现了更快的 疫苗 交付和更高的覆盖率。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为 季节性流感 计划作为大流行病防范的“温暖基地”的证据,强调了现有系统在应急响应中的价值。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,加强 季节性流感 疫苗接种 计划可以利用现有的业务能力来提高对未来流行病的准备程度。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对报告数据的依赖而受到限制,并且没有提供直接的因果证据,但研究结果得到了有和没有 季节性流感 计划的国家之间的明确比较的支持。

Investments in seasonal influenza vaccination programs pack a punch for pandemic preparedness.

Authors: Angela K. Shen, Cara Tupps, Silvia Bino, Musole Chipoya, Lia Jabidze, Ani Janashia, Milko Joksimovic, Verica Jovanovic, Nizar Maswadi, Sanjin Musa
Original abstract (English)
Low- and middle-income countries with seasonal influenza immunization programs, prior to the COVID-19 pandemic were able to deliver vaccines faster and achieve, higher coverage compared to countries without such capabilities. Middle Income, (lower- and upper-) countries participating in the Partnership for International Vaccine, Initiatives (PIVI) described influenza processes and systems that contributed to more, efficient and effective COVID-19 mass vaccination campaigns because of existing, capacities. Some routine systems were easy to pivot while others required, modifications to address the need for speed and volume when responding to the, COVID-19 pandemic. While annual influenza programs are a natural "warm base" (i.e., keeping operations such as staffing and supply chains operating at a minimum sustainable level during non-crisis periods) for honing emergency response readiness in vaccination, investments in areas more specific to pandemic preparedness remain a need. Regional variations in readiness and opportunities for cooperation should also be explored.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore how investments in seasonal influenza vaccination programs could enhance pandemic preparedness by leveraging existing infrastructure and capabilities.
Design and population
The study analyzed data from low- and middle-income countries with seasonal influenza immunization programs, comparing their vaccine delivery speed and coverage to countries without such programs prior to the COVID-19 pandemic.
Core methods
The researchers examined the operational processes and systems of seasonal influenza programs, identifying how these could be adapted or utilized for pandemic response.
Main results
Low- and middle-income countries with seasonal influenza immunization programs achieved faster vaccine delivery and higher coverage compared to those without such programs.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence that seasonal influenza programs serve as a "warm base" for pandemic preparedness, highlighting the value of existing systems in emergency response.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that strengthening seasonal influenza vaccination programs can improve readiness for future pandemics by utilizing established operational capacities.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on reported data and does not provide direct causal evidence, but the findings are supported by explicit comparisons between countries with and without seasonal influenza programs.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

H3N2 犬流感病毒中的 HA1-T138A 和 HA1-Q226L 替换有助于与人型 α2,6 连接唾液酸受体结合。

HA1-T138A and HA1-Q226L substitutions in H3N2 canine influenza virus contribute to binding to human-type α2,6-linked sialic acid receptors.
作者： Wataru Sekine, Haruhiko Kamiki, Hiroho Ishida, Hiromichi Matsugo, Kosuke Ohira, Misa Katayama, Ayano Ichikawa, Yuta Wakabayashi, Akiko Takenaka-Uema, Shin Murakami
摘要中文翻译H3N2 甲型犬流感病毒 (IAV) 源自 H3N2 禽流感病毒,已在北美和亚洲引起 暴发。尽管尚未有 H3N2 犬 IAV 传播给人类的报道,但了解受体向性变化的分子基础对于病毒 监测 和人畜共患病风险评估非常重要。为了使类禽流感病毒扩散到人类体内,病毒表面的血凝素 (HA) 糖蛋白必须获得 突变,从而能够与人类流感病毒的主要受体 α2,6 连接的唾液酸 (SA) 结合。尽管 H3N2 犬 IAV 优先结合禽病毒的主要受体 α2,3-连接的 SA,但在狗体内病毒复制过程中重复 突变 可能会导致 HA 取代,从而赋予与 α2,6-连接的 SA 的结合亲和力。在这项研究中,我们在α2,3-连接的SA敲除MDCK细胞(主要表达α2,6-连接的SA(人型细胞))中连续传代条件复制的H3N2犬IAV,以识别受体结合域中的突变。然后,我们使用反向遗传学生成了携带个体 突变 的重组病毒,并评估了它们在人型细胞中的生长特性。HA1-T138A 和 HA1-Q226L 取代显着增强了这些细胞中的病毒复制。此外,固相聚糖结合测定表明,这些 突变 增加了与 α​​2,6-连接 SA 的结合,达到与 α2,3-连接 SA 观察到的水平相当的水平。总之,这些发现表明 H3N2 犬 IAV 有可能获得 突变,从而能够有效结合 α2,6 连接的 SA。这项研究表明,HA1-T138A 和 HA1-Q226L 取代可能在 H3N2 犬 IAV 适应表达人型受体的细胞中发挥关键作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究 H3N2 犬甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 受体向性变化的分子基础,以了解其对人类造成人畜共患 传播 的可能性。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 H3N2 犬 IAV 在 α2,3 连接 SA 敲除 MDCK 细胞(主要表达 α2,6 连接 SA)中连续传代,以识别受体结合域中的 突变。然后生成携带个体 突变 的重组病毒,并评估其在人型细胞中的生长特性。
核心方法
研究人员利用反向遗传学生成具有特定 HA 突变 的重组病毒,并进行固相聚糖结合测定,以评估其与 α2,6 连接唾液酸受体的结合亲和力。
主要结果
HA1-T138A 和 HA1-Q226L 取代显着增强了人型细胞中的病毒复制。固相聚糖结合测定表明,这些 突变 增加了与 α​​2,6-连接 SA 的结合,达到与 α2,3-连接 SA 观察到的水平相当的水平。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,这些特定的 HA 取代可能使 H3N2 犬 IAV 能够适应人类型受体,强调了人畜共患 传播 的潜在分子机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果有助于了解 H3N2 犬 IAV 与人型受体结合的分子变化,这对于病毒 监测 和评估人畜共患风险非常重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到其体外性质的限制,因为它没有考虑可能影响病毒适应的体内因素。证据是中等的,因为这些发现是基于实验模型而不是直接的人类 传播 数据。

HA1-T138A and HA1-Q226L substitutions in H3N2 canine influenza virus contribute to binding to human-type α2,6-linked sialic acid receptors.

Authors: Wataru Sekine, Haruhiko Kamiki, Hiroho Ishida, Hiromichi Matsugo, Kosuke Ohira, Misa Katayama, Ayano Ichikawa, Yuta Wakabayashi, Akiko Takenaka-Uema, Shin Murakami
Original abstract (English)
The H3N2 canine influenza A virus (IAV), which originated from an H3N2 avian IAV, has caused outbreaks in North America and Asia. Although H3N2 canine IAV has not been reported to transmit to humans, understanding the molecular basis of receptor tropism changes is important for viral surveillance and zoonotic risk assessment. For avian-like IAVs to spill over into humans, the hemagglutinin (HA) glycoprotein on the viral surface must acquire mutations that enable binding to α2,6-linked sialic acids (SA), the primary receptors for human influenza viruses. Although H3N2 canine IAV preferentially binds to α2,3-linked SA, the primary receptors for avian viruses, repeated mutations during viral replication in dogs may lead to HA substitutions that confer binding affinity to α2,6-linked SA. In this study, we serially passaged conditionally replicating H3N2 canine IAV in α2,3-linked SA-knockout MDCK cells, which predominantly express α2,6-linked SA (human-type cells), to identify mutations in the receptor-binding domain. We then generated recombinant viruses carrying individual mutations using reverse genetics and evaluated their growth properties in human-type cells. The HA1-T138A and HA1-Q226L substitutions significantly enhanced viral replication in these cells. Furthermore, a solid-phase glycan-binding assay demonstrated that these mutations increased binding to α2,6-linked SA to levels comparable to those observed for α2,3-linked SA. Together, these findings indicate that H3N2 canine IAV has the potential to acquire mutations that enable efficient binding to α2,6-linked SA. This study suggests that the HA1-T138A and HA1-Q226L substitutions may play critical roles in the adaptation of H3N2 canine IAV to cells expressing human-type receptors.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the molecular basis of receptor tropism changes in H3N2 canine influenza A virus (IAV) to understand its potential for zoonotic transmission to humans.
Design and population
The study involved serial passage of H3N2 canine IAV in α2,3-linked SA-knockout MDCK cells, which predominantly express α2,6-linked SA, to identify mutations in the receptor-binding domain. Recombinant viruses carrying individual mutations were then generated and evaluated for their growth properties in human-type cells.
Core methods
The researchers used reverse genetics to generate recombinant viruses with specific HA mutations and conducted solid-phase glycan-binding assays to assess their binding affinity to α2,6-linked sialic acid receptors.
Main results
The HA1-T138A and HA1-Q226L substitutions significantly enhanced viral replication in human-type cells. A solid-phase glycan-binding assay showed that these mutations increased binding to α2,6-linked SA to levels comparable to those observed for α2,3-linked SA.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that these specific HA substitutions may enable H3N2 canine IAV to adapt to human-type receptors, highlighting a potential molecular mechanism for zoonotic transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results contribute to understanding the molecular changes that could allow H3N2 canine IAV to bind to human-type receptors, which is important for viral surveillance and assessing zoonotic risk.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its in vitro nature, as it does not account for in vivo factors that may influence viral adaptation. The evidence is moderate, as the findings are based on experimental models rather than direct human transmission data.

通过信号肽优化增强四聚体神经氨酸酶的分泌表达,在小鼠中引发针对流感感染的强大免疫力

Secretory expression of tetrameric neuraminidase enhanced by signal peptide optimization elicits robust immunity against influenza infection in mice
作者： Lisha Deng, Xianmiao Ye, Xin Chen, Xinru Hu, Xinyu Zhang, Zirong Han, Caijun Sun, Deng L, Ye X, Chen X
摘要中文翻译神经氨酸酶 (NA) 是流感病毒的一种关键表面糖蛋白,代表比血凝素 (HA) 更保守的抗原靶点,作为具有更广泛保护能力的 疫苗 的潜在靶点引起了人们的兴趣。然而,保持四聚体样结构特征的四聚体NA的低效分泌生产仍然具有挑战性。在这里,我们通过在高斯荧光素酶信号肽(Gluc-sp)的N末端引入缬氨酸和天冬酰胺残基并整合麻疹病毒磷蛋白衍生的四聚化结构域,开发了一种优化的信号肽opt-sp。在与三种常用信号肽(包括组织纤溶酶原激活剂 (tPA)、免疫球蛋白 G 信号肽 (IgG-sp) 和 Gluc-sp)的系统比较中,opt-sp 在测试的表达条件下与 NA 蛋白的相对分泌增加相关。天然 PAGE 分析进一步表明存在四聚体样 NA 物种。在小鼠中,编码 opt-sp 融合 NA 的 DNA 疫苗 比带有传统信号肽的构建体引发了更强的 NA 特异性体液和细胞 免疫应答,并提供了针对同源 A/California/04/2009 (H1N1) 攻击的改进保护。这些发现将 opt-sp 确定为改善重组 NA 分泌的有前途的信号肽候选者,并支持进一步评估这种基于 NA 的流感 疫苗 开发策略,包括异源保护的未来研究。

Secretory expression of tetrameric neuraminidase enhanced by signal peptide optimization elicits robust immunity against influenza infection in mice

Authors: Lisha Deng, Xianmiao Ye, Xin Chen, Xinru Hu, Xinyu Zhang, Zirong Han, Caijun Sun, Deng L, Ye X, Chen X
Original abstract (English)
Neuraminidase (NA), a key surface glycoprotein of influenza viruses, represents a more conserved antigenic target than hemagglutinin (HA) and has attracted interest as a potential target for vaccines with broader protective capacity. Nevertheless, the inefficient secretory production of tetrameric NA that maintain tetramer-like structural features remains challenging.Here, we developed an optimized signal peptide, opt-sp, by introducing valine and asparagine residues at the N-terminus of the Gaussia luciferase signal peptide (Gluc-sp) and incorporating a measles virus phosphoprotein-derived tetramerization domain. In a systematic comparison with three commonly used signal peptides, including tissue plasminogen activator (tPA), immunoglobulin G signal peptide (IgG-sp), and Gluc-sp, opt-sp was associated with increased relative secretion of NA protein under the tested expression conditions. Native PAGE analysis further suggested the presence of tetramer-like NA species. In mice,a DNA vaccine encoding opt-sp-fused NA elicited substantially stronger NA-specific humoral and cellular immune responses than constructs bearing conventional signal peptides and provided improved protection against homologous A/California/04/2009 (H1N1) challenge. These findings identify opt-sp as a promising signal peptide candidate for improving recombinant NA secretion and support further evaluation of this strategy for NA-based influenza vaccine development, including future studies of heterologous protection.

Neuraminidase (NA), a key surface glycoprotein of influenza viruses, represents a more conserved antigenic target than hemagglutinin (HA) and has attracted interest as a potential target for vaccines with broader protective capacity.

The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.

Main results
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a directly observed main result.

Scientific and public-health significance
In mice,a DNA vaccine encoding opt-sp-fused NA elicited substantially stronger NA-specific humoral and cellular immune responses than constructs bearing conventional signal peptides and provided improved protection against homologous A/California/04/2009 (H1N1) challenge.

封装在脂质纳米粒子中的线性 DNA 表达盒为小鼠提供针对甲型流感病毒的完全保护

Linear DNA expression cassette encapsulated in lipid nanoparticles confers complete protection against influenza A virus in mice
作者： Danh C. Lai, The N. Nguyen, Giao P. Trinh, Hiep L. X. Vu
摘要中文翻译摘要 基于 DNA 的 疫苗 已针对一系列感染原进行了开发和测试,包括甲型流感病毒 (IAV)。细菌质粒 DNA 被广泛用作 媒介,用于传递编码 疫苗 免疫原的基因。然而,传统质粒构建体中包含抗生素抗性基因引起了安全问题。通过 PCR 产生的缺乏抗生素抗性基因的线性 DNA 表达盒和细菌序列可以作为更安全的替代品 媒介。在本研究中,我们采用 IAV 小鼠模型来评估脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 封装的线性 DNA 表达盒的保护功效,该表达盒含有通过 PCR 扩增产生的 IAV H1N1 血凝素基因。为了进行比较,还构建了含有相同基因的环状质粒DNA。与 LNP 封装的环状质粒 DNA (LNP-P) 相比,LNP 封装的 DNA 盒 (LNP-L) 表现出相似的物理化学特性,例如粒径、多分散指数、zeta 电位和封装效率。然而,在细胞培养中,LNP-L 产生的转基因表达显着低于 LNP-P。与用 LNP-P 免疫的小鼠相比,用 LNP-L 免疫的小鼠出现了延迟的抗体反应,但在加强免疫后,两组之间体液和细胞介导的 免疫应答 的强度相当。此外,用 LNP-L 免疫的小鼠表现出 Th1 和 Th2 反应,与用 LNP-P 免疫的小鼠相似。重要的是,用 LNP-L 或 LNP-P 免疫的小鼠在受到致死剂量的 IAV H1N1 攻击后得到了充分的保护。这些发现表明,封装在 LNP 中的线性 DNA 表达盒代表了 疫苗 开发的一种有前途的策略。重要性 脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 封装的 DNA 疫苗 代表了快速开发 疫苗 对抗传染性病原体的有前景的平台。然而,用于传递 疫苗 免疫原的质粒 DNA 媒介 中包含的抗生素抗性基因引起了潜在的安全问题。本研究探讨了使用通过 PCR 扩增产生的线性表达盒作为传递 疫苗 免疫原的替代系统的可行性。与传统的质粒DNA相比,线性表达盒仅包含必需的元件,包括启动子、编码疫苗免疫原的基因和聚腺苷酸化信号,并且可以在无细胞系统中产生。使用甲型流感病毒小鼠模型,我们证明 LNP 封装的线性 DNA 盒可诱导体液和细胞介导的 免疫应答 并保护免疫小鼠免受致命流感病毒的攻击。因此,LNP封装的线性表达盒可以为快速应对动物传染病提供更安全的方法。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过使用线性 DNA 表达盒代替包含抗生素抗性基因的传统质粒 DNA 媒介 来评估针对甲型流感病毒的更安全的 DNA 疫苗 策略。
设计与对象
本研究使用流感A病毒小鼠模型来评估一种封装在脂质纳米颗粒(LNP)中的线性DNA表达载体的保护效果,该载体包含IAV H1N1血凝素基因。
核心方法
通过PCR扩增生产线性DNA表达载体,不包含抗生素抗性基因,并将其封装在脂质纳米颗粒中。同时构建了一种包含相同基因的环状质粒DNA作为对照。通过用致死剂量的IAV H1N1感染小鼠来测试保护效果。
主要结果
封装在LNP中的线性DNA载体(LNP-L)表现出与封装在LNP中的环状质粒DNA(LNP-P)相似的理化特性。在加强免疫后,使用LNP-L免疫的小鼠其体液免疫和细胞介导的免疫应答反应与LNP-P免疫的小鼠相当。LNP-L和LNP-P均能完全保护小鼠免受致死性流感病毒感染。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,封装在LNP中的线性DNA表达载体可能是一种有前景的疫苗开发策略,因为它们提供了更安全的替代方案,而不会降低保护效果。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果表明,这种方法可能为动物中针对季节性流感病毒的快速疫苗开发提供更安全且更高效的方式,对人类疫苗设计也具有潜在意义。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠模型,未涉及人类免疫应答或长期安全性,但证据较强,因为它在相关动物模型中直接比较了线性与环状DNA 媒介的有效性。

Linear DNA expression cassette encapsulated in lipid nanoparticles confers complete protection against influenza A virus in mice

Authors: Danh C. Lai, The N. Nguyen, Giao P. Trinh, Hiep L. X. Vu
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT DNA-based vaccines have been developed and tested against a range of infectious agents, including influenza A viruses (IAV). Bacterial plasmid DNA is widely used as a vector for delivering genes encoding vaccine immunogens. However, the inclusion of antibiotic resistance genes in conventional plasmid constructs raises safety concerns. Linear DNA expression cassettes produced via PCR that lack antibiotic resistance genes, and bacterial sequences can serve as a safer alternative vector. In this study, we employed a mouse model of IAV to evaluate the protective efficacy of a lipid nanoparticle (LNP)-encapsulated linear DNA expression cassette containing the hemagglutinin gene of IAV H1N1 produced by PCR amplification. For comparison, a circular plasmid DNA containing the same gene was also constructed. The LNP-encapsulated DNA cassette (LNP-L) exhibited similar physicochemical characteristics, such as particle size, polydispersity index, zeta potential, and encapsulation efficiency, compared to the LNP-encapsulated circular plasmid DNA (LNP-P). However, LNP-L produced significantly lower transgene expression than LNP-P in cell culture. Mice immunized with LNP-L developed a delayed onset of antibody response compared to those immunized with LNP-P, but the magnitudes of both humoral and cell-mediated immune responses were comparable between the two groups after booster immunization. Moreover, mice immunized with LNP-L exhibited both Th1 and Th2 responses, similar to those immunized with LNP-P. Importantly, mice immunized with either LNP-L or LNP-P were fully protected upon challenge with a lethal dose of IAV H1N1. These findings suggest that linear DNA expression cassettes encapsulated in LNPs represent a promising strategy for vaccine development. IMPORTANCE Lipid nanoparticle (LNP)-encapsulated DNA vaccines represent a promising platform for the rapid development of vaccines against infectious pathogens. However, the inclusion of an antibiotic-resistance gene in plasmid DNA vectors used to deliver vaccine immunogens raises potential safety concerns. This study explores the feasibility of using a linear expression cassette produced via PCR amplification as an alternative system for delivering vaccine immunogens. Compared with conventional plasmid DNA, the linear expression cassette contains only essential elements, including a promoter, the gene encoding the vaccine immunogen, and a polyadenylation signal, and can be produced in a cell-free system. Using a mouse model for influenza A virus, we demonstrate that an LNP-encapsulated linear DNA cassette induces humoral and cell-mediated immune responses and protects immunized mice against a lethal influenza virus challenge. Thus, the LNP-encapsulated linear expression cassette may provide a safer approach for rapid responses to infectious diseases in animals.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate a safer DNA vaccine strategy for influenza A virus by using a linear DNA expression cassette instead of conventional plasmid DNA vectors, which include antibiotic resistance genes.
Design and population
The study used a mouse model of influenza A virus to assess the protective efficacy of a lipid nanoparticle (LNP)-encapsulated linear DNA expression cassette containing the hemagglutinin gene of IAV H1N1.
Core methods
Linear DNA expression cassettes were produced via PCR amplification without antibiotic resistance genes, and were encapsulated in lipid nanoparticles. A circular plasmid DNA containing the same gene was also constructed for comparison. The protective efficacy was tested by challenging mice with a lethal dose of IAV H1N1.
Main results
The LNP-encapsulated linear DNA cassette (LNP-L) showed similar physicochemical properties to the LNP-encapsulated circular plasmid DNA (LNP-P). Mice immunized with LNP-L had a delayed onset of antibody response but comparable humoral and cell-mediated immune responses after booster immunization. Both LNP-L and LNP-P fully protected mice against a lethal influenza virus challenge.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that linear DNA expression cassettes encapsulated in LNPs could be a promising strategy for vaccine development, as they offer a safer alternative without compromising protective efficacy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results indicate that this approach may provide a safer and more efficient method for rapid vaccine development against seasonal influenza viruses in animals, with potential implications for human vaccine design.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a mouse model and does not address human immune responses or long-term safety, but the evidence is strong as it directly compares the efficacy of linear and circular DNA vectors in a relevant animal model.

N6-甲基腺苷调控流感A病毒mRNA稳定性,但很少出现在基因组RNA上

N6-methyladenosine regulates Influenza A virus mRNA stability yet is rarely found on genomic RNA
作者： Hsiu-Yi Wu, Wan-Ju Tung, Ramreishang Wungmaiwo, Pei-Yi Su, Hung-Wei Hsu, Ankit Gupta, Brian N. Papas, Marcos Morgan, Wen‐Chun Liu, I-Hsuan Wang
摘要中文翻译先前研究发现,所有形式的流感A病毒(IAV)RNA中普遍存在N6-甲基腺苷(m6A甲基化),m6A被发现对病毒复制、致病性以及病毒RNA包装至关重要。在这里,我们应用最新的定量技术重新审视IAV反义基因组RNA上的甲基化图谱。令人意外的是,通过超高效液相色谱-串联质谱(UPLC-MS/MS)分析从IAV病毒颗粒中提取的基因组RNA时,无论其来源于人类细胞还是鸡卵,我们均检测到非常少的m6A。同样,纳米孔直接RNA测序也检测到所有病毒基因组RNA片段中m6A的整体出现率和化学计量比(通常<5%)较低,与病毒mRNA上约20-30%的丰富m6A位点形成对比。通过使用糖醛酸和亚硝酸盐介导的未甲基化腺苷脱氨化(GLORI)进行交叉验证,确认了病毒mRNA上存在多个m6A位点,但基因组RNA上仅有极少的m6A位点。这种基因组RNA上m6A的缺乏使得m6A不太可能参与病毒RNA包装。敲低或药理学抑制m6A甲基转移酶METTL3以及阅读蛋白YTHDF2均降低了病毒mRNA水平和感染性病毒颗粒的产生,其中YTHDF2促进病毒mRNA的稳定性。因此,IAV转录本上的m6A确实具有促病毒作用,但其功能相关的甲基化水平主要出现在mRNA上,而非基因组RNA上。最后,我们提供了小分子抑制剂抗病毒药物用于METTL3的初步概念验证,并提出针对m6A的抗病毒药物主要会影响IAV复制过程中的细胞内基因表达阶段。

问题与背景
先前研究发现,所有形式的流感A病毒(IAV)RNA中普遍存在N6-甲基腺苷(m6A甲基化),m6A被发现对病毒复制、致病性以及病毒RNA包装至关重要。
设计与对象
在这里,我们应用最新的定量技术重新审视IAV反义基因组RNA上的甲基化图谱。
核心方法
同样,纳米孔直接RNA测序也检测到所有病毒基因组RNA片段中m6A的整体出现率和化学计量比(通常<5%)较低,与病毒mRNA上约20-30%的丰富m6A位点形成对比。
主要结果
通过使用糖醛酸和亚硝酸盐介导的未甲基化腺苷脱氨化(GLORI)进行交叉验证,确认了病毒mRNA上存在多个m6A位点,但基因组RNA上仅有极少的m6A位点。
解释与创新
这种基因组RNA上m6A的缺乏使得m6A不太可能参与病毒RNA包装。
科研与公卫意义
m6A 甲基转移酶 METTL3 以及阅读器蛋白 YTHDF2 的敲低或药理抑制均会降低病毒 mRNA 水平和感染性病毒颗粒的产生,而 YTHDF2 会促进病毒 mRNA 的稳定性。
局限与证据强度
因此,IAV 转录本上 m6A 的存在确实是前病毒,但在功能相关水平上甲基化的是 mRNA,而不是基因组 RNA。

N6-methyladenosine regulates Influenza A virus mRNA stability yet is rarely found on genomic RNA

Authors: Hsiu-Yi Wu, Wan-Ju Tung, Ramreishang Wungmaiwo, Pei-Yi Su, Hung-Wei Hsu, Ankit Gupta, Brian N. Papas, Marcos Morgan, Wen‐Chun Liu, I-Hsuan Wang
Original abstract (English)
Previous studies have found widespread N6-methyladenosine (m6A methylation) on all forms of Influenza A virus (IAV) RNA, with m6A found critical for viral replication, pathogenicity as well as viral RNA packaging. Here we applied the latest quantitative technologies to revisit the methylation landscape on the anti-sense genomic RNA of IAV. Unexpectedly, upon Ultra-Performance Liquid Chromatography-Tandem Mass Spectrometry (UPLC-MS/MS) analysis of IAV virion -extracted genomic RNA, we detected very little m6A regardless of production from human cells or chicken eggs. Concordantly, Nanopore direct RNA sequencing also detected an overall low occurrence and stoichiometry (generally <5%) of m6A across all viral genomic RNA segments, compared with abundant m6A sites on viral mRNAs at ~20-30% m6A. Cross validation with glyoxal- and nitrite-mediated deamination of unmethylated adenosines (GLORI) confirmed multiple m6A sites on viral mRNA yet very few m6A on the genomic RNA. This paucity of m6A on genomic RNA makes it unlikely that m6A contributes to viral RNA packaging. Knockdown or pharmacological inhibition of the m6A methyltransferase METTL3 as well as the reader protein YTHDF2 both reduced viral mRNA levels and infectious viral particle production, with YTHDF2 promoting viral mRNA stability. Thus, the presence of m6A on IAV transcripts is indeed proviral, yet it is the mRNAs instead of genomic RNAs that are methylated at functionally relevant levels. Lastly, we provide proof of concept that a METTL3 small molecule inhibitor can be antiviral, and propose that m6A-targeted antivirals would mainly impact the intracellular gene expression phase of IAV replication.

关于冠状病毒病2019年(COVID-19)和季节性流感疫苗和疫苗接种在一般人群和医疗专业人员中的态度和做法

ATTITUDES AND PRACTICES TOWARDS COVID-19 AND SEASONAL FLU VACCINES AND VACCINATION IN THE GENERAL POPULATION AND AMONG MEDICAL PROFESSIONALS
作者： Katarina Petković, Dušanka M. Krajnović, Andijana Milošević-Georgijev, B. Filipić, Andjela Marinković, Valentina Marinković
摘要中文翻译冠状病毒病2019年(COVID-19)大流行促使开发了多种类型的疫苗,其中基于信使核糖核酸(mRNA)的疫苗代表了重要的突破。本研究旨在评估一般人群和具有医疗背景的个体对冠状病毒病2019年(COVID-19)和季节性流感 疫苗接种的态度。设计了一份问卷,用于收集参与者的社会人口学数据、个人健康信息、疫苗接种状态以及他们对疫苗的态度和知识。典型参与者是一名在城市地区与他人同住的受过高等教育的女性雇员,报告其总体健康状况良好。典型参与者已接种至少两剂的卫星V(Sputnik V)疫苗。参与者普遍对定期接种冠状病毒病2019年(COVID-19)疫苗接种表现出积极态度。结果显示,具有和不具有医疗背景的参与者在疫苗选择、是否决定接种第三剂或季节性流感 疫苗接种方面没有明显差异。同样,具有和不具有医疗背景的受访者在对阿斯利康(AstraZeneca)疫苗或强制性冠状病毒病2019年(COVID-19)疫苗接种的态度方面也没有明显差异。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
冠状病毒病2019年(COVID-19)大流行促使开发了多种类型的疫苗,其中基于信使核糖核酸(mRNA)的疫苗代表了重要的突破。
设计与对象
本研究旨在评估一般人群和具有医疗背景的个体对冠状病毒病2019年(COVID-19)和季节性流感 疫苗接种的态度。
核心方法
参与者普遍对定期接种冠状病毒病2019年(COVID-19)疫苗接种表现出积极态度。
主要结果
结果显示,具有和不具有医疗背景的参与者在疫苗选择、是否决定接种第三剂或季节性流感 疫苗接种方面没有明显差异。
解释与创新
典型参与者已接种至少两剂的卫星V(Sputnik V)疫苗。
科研与公卫意义
同样,具有和不具有医疗背景的受访者在对阿斯利康(AstraZeneca)疫苗或强制性冠状病毒病2019年(COVID-19)疫苗接种的态度方面也没有明显差异。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取基于检索到的全文证据。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

ATTITUDES AND PRACTICES TOWARDS COVID-19 AND SEASONAL FLU VACCINES AND VACCINATION IN THE GENERAL POPULATION AND AMONG MEDICAL PROFESSIONALS

Authors: Katarina Petković, Dušanka M. Krajnović, Andijana Milošević-Georgijev, B. Filipić, Andjela Marinković, Valentina Marinković
Original abstract (English)
The COVID-19 pandemic has led to the development of several types of vaccines, among which messenger RNA (mRNA)-based vaccines have represented a significant breakthrough. This study aimed to evaluate attitudes towards COVID-19 and seasonal influenza vaccination among the general population and individuals with a medical background. A questionnaire was developed to collect sociodemographic data, personal health information, vaccination status, and information on participants’ attitudes towards and knowledge of vaccines. The profile of the typical participant was an employed woman with tertiary education, living with another person in an urban area and reporting generally good health. The typical participant had received at least two doses of the Sputnik V vaccine. Participants generally expressed favourable attitudes towards regular COVID-19 vaccination. The results showed no apparent differences between participants with and without a medical background regarding vaccine choice, the decision to receive a third dose, or seasonal influenza vaccination. Similarly, no apparent differences were observed in attitudes towards the AstraZeneca vaccine or mandatory COVID-19 vaccination between respondents with and without a medical background.

Limitations and evidence strength

干预项目对伊拉克大学生对季节性流感 疫苗知识和态度的影响

The impact of the intervention program on students’ knowledge and attitudes towards seasonal influenza vaccine in Iraq
作者： Rawaa Kamel Abd, Vinoth Raman
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估干预项目在提高伊拉克大学生对季节性流感 疫苗的知识和态度方面的有效性,因为季节性流感在全球范围内对健康构成重大风险,而疫苗接种是一种关键的预防措施。
设计与对象
采用准实验设计,在伊拉克南部的Thi-Qar大学(TQU)六个月内,通过简单随机抽签选取的六所学院中,采用便利抽样方法招募了204名学生(干预组102人,对照组102人)。
核心方法
使用结构化的教育计划、小册子以及前后测试来衡量学生的知识和态度。使用独立样本和配对样本 t 检验来分析数据,显着性水平为 p < 0.05。
主要结果
术前测试中,干预组有40.2%的学生知识水平较低(M ± SD = 1.68 ± 0.616);术后测试中,55.9%的学生知识水平较高(M ± SD = 1.73 ± 0.448)。在态度方面,干预后73.5%的参与者报告了积极的态度,而26.5%报告了消极的态度,这比术前测试时的60.8%消极态度有所下降。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,干预项目导致了与季节性流感 疫苗相关的知识和态度的显著变化(p < 0.001),突显了结构化教育干预在提高伊拉克大学生对疫苗接种接受度方面的有效性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,以大学为基础的流感 疫苗接种 项目可以提高 疫苗 的覆盖率并减少流感相关疾病,为伊拉克有针对性的公共卫生战略提供基础。
局限与证据强度
本研究使用了便利样本和准实验设计,这可能限制了结果的普遍性。然而,结果得到了显著性水平为p < 0.05的统计分析的支持,表明证据强度中等。

The impact of the intervention program on students’ knowledge and attitudes towards seasonal influenza vaccine in Iraq

Authors: Rawaa Kamel Abd, Vinoth Raman
Original abstract (English)
This study demonstrated that a structured educational intervention significantly improved university students' knowledge and attitudes towards the seasonal influenza vaccine in Iraq, leading to increased vaccination uptake. The purpose of the present study was to evaluate the effectiveness of an intervention program in improving university students’ knowledge and attitudes regarding the seasonal influenza vaccine. Materials and methods A quasi-experimental design was employed, with convenience sampling of 204 students (102 intervention, 102 control) over a period of six months at Thi-Qar University (TQU) in southern Iraq, across six colleges selected via a simple random lottery. Results At pre-test, 40.2% of the intervention group had low knowledge (M ± SD = 1.68 ± 0.616); at post-test, 55.9% had high knowledge (M ± SD = 1.73 ± 0.448). Conclusion Significant change in knowledge and attitudes in relation to seasonal influenza vaccine following intervention (p < 0.001). For best results, children ages 6 months to 8 years need to receive two doses of the influenza vaccine during their first immunization season, spaced at least 4 weeks apart [9, 10]. (2016) conducted a study amongst medical students in Australia, which found that knowledge and attitudes were strongly linked to vaccination uptake, highlighting the importance of structured learning in improving vaccine uptake [12]. Article summary AI generated Seasonal influenza poses significant health risks globally, making vaccination a crucial preventive measure. Abstract Background & aim Influenza is a serious viral respiratory disease, and vaccination is the primary means of prevention. Students’ knowledge and attitudes were measured using a structured educational program, brochures, and pre- and post-tests. Independen...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of an intervention program in improving university students’ knowledge and attitudes towards the seasonal influenza vaccine in Iraq, as seasonal influenza poses significant health risks globally and vaccination is a crucial preventive measure.
Design and population
A quasi-experimental design was employed, with convenience sampling of 204 students (102 intervention, 102 control) over a period of six months at Thi-Qar University (TQU) in southern Iraq, across six colleges selected via a simple random lottery.
Core methods
Students’ knowledge and attitudes were measured using a structured educational program, brochures, and pre- and post-tests. Independent- and paired-samples t-tests were used to analyze the data, with a significance level of p < 0.05.
Main results
At pre-test, 40.2% of the intervention group had low knowledge (M ± SD = 1.68 ± 0.616); at post-test, 55.9% had high knowledge (M ± SD = 1.73 ± 0.448). In terms of attitudes, 73.5% of participants reported positive attitudes after the intervention, while 26.5% reported negative attitudes, a decrease from pre-test, where 60.8% stated that they had negative attitudes.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that the intervention program led to a significant change in knowledge and attitudes in relation to the seasonal influenza vaccine (p < 0.001), highlighting the effectiveness of structured educational interventions in improving vaccine acceptance among university students in Iraq.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that university-based influenza vaccination programs could improve vaccine coverage and reduce influenza-related disease, providing a basis for targeted public health strategies in Iraq.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study used a convenience sample and a quasi-experimental design, which may limit generalizability. However, the results are supported by statistical analysis with a significance level of p < 0.05, indicating moderate evidence strength.

重组流感疫苗与标准剂量流感疫苗在严重肥胖成人中的比较:一项随机临床试验。

Recombinant vs Standard Influenza Vaccine in Adults With Severe Obesity: A Randomized Clinical Trial.
作者： Paul Loubet, Sébastien Czernichow, Caroline Giboin, Ariane Sultan, Thomas Guimard, Emmanuel Disse, Marie-Pierre Tavolacci, Ines Ben Ghezala, Séverine Ledoux, Cécile Janssen
摘要中文翻译背景:严重肥胖个体面临严重的流感风险,且可能对流感免疫应答存在损害。与基于鸡蛋的标准剂量流感疫苗(SD)相比,重组流感疫苗(RIV)可能提供更强的保护作用,但针对这一高危人群的数据有限。方法:AP-HP FLUO试验(NCT05409612)是一项在法国15个中心进行的开放标签、随机临床试验(2022年11月至2023年3月),随访期为6个月。纳入的受试者为BMI≥35 kg/m2的成年人,按1:1的比例随机分配接受RIV或SD,采用中心、年龄(<50岁 vs ≥50岁)和BMI(<40 kg/m2 vs ≥40 kg/m2)进行最小化随机。主要结局是接种后28天针对4种流感病毒株的几何平均血凝抑制(HAI)滴度(GMT)的比值(RIV/SD)。同时评估了安全性和反应原性。结果:共纳入206名受试者(RIV组104人,SD组102人)。中位年龄为50岁,60.2%为女性,中位BMI为41.0 kg/m2。在第28天,RIV组对A/H1N1(比值1.6;95%置信区间,1.1-2.3)、A/H3N2(比值2.0;95%置信区间,1.3-3.2)和B/Yamagata(比值1.3;95%置信区间,1.0-1.8)的GMT比值优于SD组,但对B/Victoria(比值0.9;95%置信区间,0.6-1.3)则无显著差异。不同年龄和BMI组之间效果无显著差异。到第180天时,滴度无显著差异。两组的反应原性和安全性特征相似。结论:在严重肥胖成人中,RIV比基于鸡蛋的标准剂量疫苗诱导了更强的短期体液免疫应答,提示该疫苗可能对这一易感人群的流感预防具有额外益处。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
严重肥胖的个体患严重流感的风险增加,并且可能损害 免疫应答 至 疫苗接种。该研究旨在评估 免疫应答 重组流感 疫苗 (RIV) 与标准剂量流感 疫苗 (SD) 在严重肥胖成人中的作用。
设计与对象
AP-HP FLUO试验是一项随机临床试验,于2022年11月至2023年3月在法国15个中心进行,随访6个月。BMI ≥35 kg/m2 的成年人按 1:1 随机分配接受 RIV 或 SD,采用按中心、年龄(<50 岁 vs ≥50 岁)和 BMI(<40 vs ≥40 kg/m2)最小化方法。
核心方法
该研究评估了 疫苗接种 后 28 天 4 种流感病毒株的几何平均血凝素抑制 (HAI) 滴度。还评估了安全性和反应原性。
主要结果
A total of 206 participants were included (104 RIV, 102 SD). At Day 28, GMT ratios favored RIV for A/H1N1 (1.6; 95% CI, 1.1-2.3), A/H3N2 (2.0; 95% CI, 1.3-3.2), and B/Yamagata (1.3; 95% CI, 1.0-1.8), but not for B/Victoria (0.9; 95% CI, 0.6-1.3). By Day 180, titers did not differ significantly.
解释与创新
作者解释说,在严重肥胖的成年人中,RIV 比 SD 更能引发更强的短期体液 免疫应答,这表明对于该人群的流感预防具有潜在的额外益处。这一发现凸显了 RIV 在弱势群体中的免疫原性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为针对严重肥胖成人(流感并发症的高危人群)制定 疫苗接种 策略提供参考。他们认为 RIV 可以为这一人群提供更好的保护。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其随访期短且缺乏长期疗效数据而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为结果是基于样本量相对较小和开放标签设计的单一试验。

Recombinant vs Standard Influenza Vaccine in Adults With Severe Obesity: A Randomized Clinical Trial.

Authors: Paul Loubet, Sébastien Czernichow, Caroline Giboin, Ariane Sultan, Thomas Guimard, Emmanuel Disse, Marie-Pierre Tavolacci, Ines Ben Ghezala, Séverine Ledoux, Cécile Janssen
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Individuals with severe obesity are at increased risk of severe influenza and may have impaired immune responses to vaccination. Recombinant influenza vaccine (RIV) may provide enhanced protection compared with egg-based standard-dose influenza vaccine (SD), but data in this high-risk population are limited. METHODS: The AP-HP FLUO trial (NCT05409612) was an open-label, randomized clinical trial conducted in 15 centers in France (November 2022-March 2023) with 6 months of follow-up. Adults with BMI ≥35 kg/m2 were randomized 1:1 to receive RIV or SD, using minimization by center, age (<50 vs ≥50 years), and BMI (<40 vs ≥40 kg/m2). The primary outcome was the ratio (RIV/SD) of geometric mean hemagglutinin-inhibition (HAI) titers (GMTs) for 4 influenza strains 28 days after vaccination. Safety and reactogenicity were also assessed. RESULTS: A total of 206 participants were included (104 RIV, 102 SD). Median age was 50 years, 60.2% were women, and median BMI was 41.0 kg/m2. At Day 28, GMT ratios favored RIV for A/H1N1 (1.6; 95% CI, 1.1-2.3), A/H3N2 (2.0; 95% CI, 1.3-3.2), and B/Yamagata (1.3; 95% CI, 1.0-1.8), but not for B/Victoria (0.9; 95% CI, 0.6-1.3). The effect did not vary significantly across the different age and BMI groups. By Day 180, titers did not differ significantly. Reactogenicity and safety profiles were similar between groups. CONCLUSIONS: In adults with severe obesity, RIV elicited stronger short-term humoral immune responses than an egg-based standard-dose vaccine, suggesting potential additional benefit for influenza prevention in this vulnerable population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Individuals with severe obesity are at increased risk of severe influenza and may have impaired immune responses to vaccination. The study aimed to evaluate the immune response to recombinant influenza vaccine (RIV) versus standard-dose influenza vaccine (SD) in adults with severe obesity.
Design and population
The AP-HP FLUO trial was a randomized clinical trial conducted in 15 centers in France from November 2022 to March 2023, with 6 months of follow-up. Adults with BMI ≥35 kg/m2 were randomized 1:1 to receive RIV or SD, using minimization by center, age (<50 vs ≥50 years), and BMI (<40 vs ≥40 kg/m2).
Core methods
The study assessed the geometric mean hemagglutinin-inhibition (HAI) titers for 4 influenza strains 28 days after vaccination. Safety and reactogenicity were also evaluated.
Main results
A total of 206 participants were included (104 RIV, 102 SD). At Day 28, GMT ratios favored RIV for A/H1N1 (1.6; 95% CI, 1.1-2.3), A/H3N2 (2.0; 95% CI, 1.3-3.2), and B/Yamagata (1.3; 95% CI, 1.0-1.8), but not for B/Victoria (0.9; 95% CI, 0.6-1.3). By Day 180, titers did not differ significantly.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that RIV elicited stronger short-term humoral immune responses than SD in adults with severe obesity, suggesting potential additional benefit for influenza prevention in this population. This finding highlights the immunogenicity of RIV in a vulnerable group.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of vaccination strategies for adults with severe obesity, a high-risk group for influenza complications. They suggest that RIV could offer improved protection in this population.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its short follow-up period and the lack of long-term efficacy data. The evidence is moderate, as the results are based on a single trial with a relatively small sample size and open-label design.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

2024-2025 年季节流感 疫苗 在中国深圳学龄儿童中的真实有效性:一项测试阴性设计研究。

Real-world effectiveness of the influenza vaccine among school-aged children in Shenzhen, China, 2024-2025 season: A test-negative design study.
作者： Jianhua Lu, Zhiyao Ouyang, Fang Huang, Xu Xie, Xin Wang, Xiaoliang Wu, Biyi Hu, Jiatong Sun, Min Jiang, Ying Sun
摘要中文翻译目的:全面评估流感 疫苗 在双高峰季节对学龄儿童的现实有效性,特别检查亚型特异性保护、年龄变化、保护随时间的持久性以及重复前季节 疫苗 的影响。方法:采用测试阴性设计来评估 疫苗 在深圳学龄儿童中的有效性(VE)。根据问卷数据和实验室检测,实验室确诊的流感患者被归类为病例,检测阴性患者作为对照。应用多变量逻辑回归来比较病例和对照之间 疫苗接种 的几率。结果:在 1669 名符合条件的参与者(463 例病例和 1206 名测试阴性对照)中,针对就医流感的总体调整后 VE 为 46.36%(95% CI:29.23-59.34%)。VE 随年龄组的不同而不同,主要在年幼的儿童中观察到。在疫苗接种后14-119天内观察到显着的保护,在120-239天期间变得不显着,并在240-299天的夏季高峰期间重新出现。仅前一赛季的 疫苗接种 无法提供显着的残余保护,而连续两个赛季重复使用 疫苗接种 会导致本赛季的有效性降低。亚型特异性 VE 针对甲型流感 (H1N1) 为 43.48% (95% CI: 18.00-61.04%),针对流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 为 34.42% (95% CI: 0.55-56.76%),针对 B/Victoria 为 56.95% (95% CI: 7.68-79.93%)血统。冬春高峰期间,VE为49.84%(95% CI:26.90-65.59%)。结论:2024-2025 年季节,流感 疫苗 对深圳学龄儿童流感相关门诊就诊具有中等效果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估流感 疫苗 在双高峰季节对学龄儿童的现实有效性,重点关注亚型特异性保护、年龄变化、保护随时间的持久性以及重复前一季节 疫苗 的影响。由于流感活动的双峰性质以及了解 疫苗 在这一弱势群体中的有效性的需要,这一点很重要。
设计与对象
2024-2025年流感季节期间,在中国深圳进行了一项测试阴性设计研究,涉及1669名符合条件的参与者,其中包括463名实验室确诊的流感病例和1206名测试阴性对照。该研究主要针对学龄儿童。
核心方法
该研究利用问卷数据和实验室检测将实验室确诊的流感患者分类为病例,将检测阴性的患者分类为对照。应用多变量逻辑回归来比较病例和对照之间 疫苗接种 的几率。
主要结果
在 1669 名符合条件的参与者中,疫苗 针对就医流感的总体调整后有效性 (VE) 为 46.36%(95% CI:29.23-59.34%)。VE 因年龄组而异,主要见于年幼的儿童。冬春高峰期间,VE为49.84%(95% CI:26.90-65.59%)。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解读为,疫苗 对 2024-2025 年季节期间深圳学龄儿童流感相关门诊就诊的效果中等。新颖之处在于疫苗在双高峰季节的有效性评估及其在不同年龄段的变化。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要采取有针对性的 疫苗接种 方法并监测不同年龄组的有效性,从而为学龄儿童(特别是流感双峰季节地区)的流感 疫苗接种 公共卫生策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对自我报告数据的依赖和潜在的混杂因素的限制而受到限制。由于测试阴性设计的观察性质以及缺乏长期随访数据,证据强度中等。

Real-world effectiveness of the influenza vaccine among school-aged children in Shenzhen, China, 2024-2025 season: A test-negative design study.

Authors: Jianhua Lu, Zhiyao Ouyang, Fang Huang, Xu Xie, Xin Wang, Xiaoliang Wu, Biyi Hu, Jiatong Sun, Min Jiang, Ying Sun
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To comprehensively evaluate the real-world effectiveness of the influenza vaccine among school-aged children across a dual-peak season, specifically examining subtype-specific protection, age variations, durability of protection over time, and the impact of repeated prior-season vaccination. METHODS: A test-negative design was used to assess vaccine effectiveness (VE) among school-aged children in Shenzhen. Based on questionnaire data and laboratory testing, patients with laboratory-confirmed influenza were classified as cases, while test-negative patients served as controls. Multivariable logistic regression was applied to compare the odds of vaccination between cases and controls. RESULTS: Among 1669 eligible participants (463 cases and 1206 test-negative controls), the overall adjusted VE against medically attended influenza was 46.36% (95% CI: 29.23-59.34%). VE differed across age groups and was mainly observed in younger children. Significant protection was observed within 14-119 days post-vaccination, became non-significant during 120-239 days, and re-emerged during the summer peak at 240-299 days. Prior-season vaccination alone provided no significant residual protection, while repeated vaccination across two consecutive seasons was associated with reduced effectiveness in the current season. Subtype-specific VE was 43.48% (95% CI: 18.00-61.04%) against influenza A(H1N1), 34.42% (95% CI: 0.55-56.76%) against influenza A(H3N2), and 56.95% (95% CI: 7.68-79.93%) against the B/Victoria lineage. During the winter-spring peak, VE was 49.84% (95% CI: 26.90-65.59%). CONCLUSIONS: Influenza vaccine provided moderate effectiveness against influenza-associated outpatient visits among school-aged children in Shenzhen during the 2024-2025 season.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the real-world effectiveness of the influenza vaccine among school-aged children during a dual-peak season, focusing on subtype-specific protection, age variations, protection durability over time, and the impact of repeated prior-season vaccination. This is important due to the dual-peak nature of influenza activity and the need to understand vaccine effectiveness in this vulnerable population.
Design and population
A test-negative design study was conducted in Shenzhen, China, during the 2024-2025 influenza season, involving 1669 eligible participants, including 463 cases with laboratory-confirmed influenza and 1206 test-negative controls. The study focused on school-aged children.
Core methods
The study used questionnaire data and laboratory testing to classify patients with laboratory-confirmed influenza as cases and test-negative patients as controls. Multivariable logistic regression was applied to compare the odds of vaccination between cases and controls.
Main results
Among 1669 eligible participants, the overall adjusted vaccine effectiveness (VE) against medically attended influenza was 46.36% (95% CI: 29.23-59.34%). VE varied across age groups and was mainly observed in younger children. During the winter-spring peak, VE was 49.84% (95% CI: 26.90-65.59%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating moderate vaccine effectiveness against influenza-associated outpatient visits among school-aged children in Shenzhen during the 2024-2025 season. The novelty lies in the assessment of vaccine effectiveness during a dual-peak season and its variation across age groups.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies for influenza vaccination in school-aged children, particularly in regions with dual-peak influenza seasons, by highlighting the need for targeted vaccination approaches and monitoring effectiveness across different age groups.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported data and the potential for confounding factors. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the test-negative design and the lack of long-term follow-up data.

含有关键抗原 突变 的共识 H3N2 HA 免疫原可引发广谱中和反应

Consensus H3N2 HA immunogens incorporating key antigenic mutations elicit broad-spectrum neutralising responses
作者： Yimeng An, Meina Cai, Chenyan Zhao, Mengyi Zhang, Xi Wu, Weijin Huang, Xu Zhou, Youchun Wang
摘要中文翻译季节性流感 A/H3N2 由于其快速抗原 进化 造成了重大的公共卫生负担,因此需要频繁更新 疫苗。本研究使用基于假病毒的中和测定和抗原制图系统地分析了 14 个 H3N2 疫苗 毒株(2000-2022 年)的抗原 进化。疫苗菌株表现出交叉中和模式,相邻年份的菌株之间具有更高的反应性,形成六个抗原簇。关键氨基酸 突变(例如 144 K、189N)被确定为抗原变异的关键驱动因素,对中和敏感性具有聚类特异性影响。基于这些发现,通过在关键位点结合突变设计了共有HA免疫原(consensus up-HA)。该免疫原对代表 2011 年至 2024 年流行毒株(包括最新变种)的假病毒具有广谱中和作用。这些结果凸显了靶向 HA 修饰在开发广泛交叉反应的 H3N2 疫苗 候选药物方面的潜力,为不断进化的 H3N2 菌株提供更广泛、更持久的中和活性。

问题与背景
季节性流感 A/H3N2 由于其快速抗原 进化 造成了重大的公共卫生负担,因此需要频繁更新 疫苗。
设计与对象

核心方法
疫苗菌株表现出交叉中和模式,相邻年份的菌株之间具有更高的反应性,形成六个抗原簇。
主要结果
关键氨基酸 突变(例如 144 K、189N)被确定为抗原变异的关键驱动因素,对中和敏感性具有聚类特异性影响。
解释与创新
基于这些发现,通过在关键位点结合突变设计了共有HA免疫原(consensus up-HA)。
科研与公卫意义
该免疫原对代表 2011 年至 2024 年流行毒株(包括最新变种)的假病毒具有广谱中和作用。
局限与证据强度
这些结果凸显了靶向 HA 修饰在开发广泛交叉反应的 H3N2 疫苗 候选药物方面的潜力,为不断进化的 H3N2 菌株提供更广泛、更持久的中和活性。

Consensus H3N2 HA immunogens incorporating key antigenic mutations elicit broad-spectrum neutralising responses

Authors: Yimeng An, Meina Cai, Chenyan Zhao, Mengyi Zhang, Xi Wu, Weijin Huang, Xu Zhou, Youchun Wang
Original abstract (English)
Seasonal influenza A/H3N2 poses a significant public health burden due to its rapid antigenic evolution, necessitating frequent vaccine updates. This study systematically analysed the antigenic evolution of 14 H3N2 vaccine strains (2000-2022) using pseudovirus-based neutralisation assays and antigenic cartography. Vaccine strains exhibited cross-neutralisation patterns, with higher reactivity among strains from adjacent years, forming six antigenic clusters. Key amino acid mutations (e.g., 144 K, 189N) were identified as critical drivers of antigenic variation, with clustering-specific effects on neutralisation sensitivity. Based on these findings, a consensus HA immunogen (consensus up-HA) was designed by combining mutations at key sites. This immunogen demonstrated broad-spectrum neutralisation against pseudoviruses representing circulating strains from 2011 to 2024, including recent variants. These results highlight the potential of targeted HA modifications to develop broadly cross-reactive H3N2 vaccine candidate, offering broader and more durable neutralising activity against evolving H3N2 strains.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Seasonal influenza A/H3N2 poses a significant public health burden due to its rapid antigenic evolution, necessitating frequent vaccine updates.
Design and population
This study systematically analysed the antigenic evolution of 14 H3N2 vaccine strains (2000-2022) using pseudovirus-based neutralisation assays and antigenic cartography.
Core methods
Vaccine strains exhibited cross-neutralisation patterns, with higher reactivity among strains from adjacent years, forming six antigenic clusters.
Main results
Key amino acid mutations (e.g., 144 K, 189N) were identified as critical drivers of antigenic variation, with clustering-specific effects on neutralisation sensitivity.
Interpretation and novelty
Based on these findings, a consensus HA immunogen (consensus up-HA) was designed by combining mutations at key sites.
Scientific and public-health significance
This immunogen demonstrated broad-spectrum neutralisation against pseudoviruses representing circulating strains from 2011 to 2024, including recent variants.
Limitations and evidence strength
These results highlight the potential of targeted HA modifications to develop broadly cross-reactive H3N2 vaccine candidate, offering broader and more durable neutralising activity against evolving H3N2 strains.

疗养院居民急性呼吸道病毒感染的临床影响。

Clinical Impact of Acute Respiratory Virus Infections in Nursing Home Residents.
作者： Lisa M. Kolodziej, Laura C Grootegoed, Menno D de Jong, Hertogh CMPM, Froukje Taat, Maureen Huisman, Rosanne Peuscher, Alvin X. Han, Laura W. van Buul, Sacha D. Kuil
摘要中文翻译目的:调查荷兰疗养院(NH)居民的疑似急性呼吸道感染(ARI)风险以及一系列呼吸道病毒的临床影响。设计:前瞻性 队列研究 研究(2023 年 3 月至 2025 年 5 月)。地点和参与者:来自 15 个荷兰 NH 的居民因怀疑患有急性呼吸道感染而被纳入研究。方法:在第 0、14 和 28 天收集临床数据。对残留的鼻咽和口咽拭子进行广泛的呼吸道病毒检测。包括季节性和 NH 水平随机效应的贝叶斯多级逻辑模型估计了疑似 ARI 风险。通过病毒比较相对临床结果。结果:在 3195 名居民中,发生了 526 起疑似急性呼吸道感染事件。估计疑似 ARI 风险为 10.2%(95% 可信区间,1.7-43.8)。在 291 次发作中鉴定出病毒:严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2(SARS-CoV-2;29.0%)、甲型流感病毒(23.6%)、鼻病毒/肠道病毒(18.8%)、呼吸道合胞病毒(11.8%)、人类偏肺病毒(hMPV;6.4%)、副流感病毒(5.1%)和人类冠状病毒(5.4%)。hMPV 和呼吸道合胞病毒往往与中度或重度疾病的较高几率相关,并且 hMPV 与卧床时间增加 4 倍相关。甲型流感与恢复延迟、需氧量以及继发细菌性肺炎的风险增加 3 倍相关。SARS-CoV-2 病例在第 14 天时预计康复的几率较高,并且在 ARI 发作期间在床上花费的额外时间较少。结论和意义:流感仍然是新罕布什尔州居民 ARI 负担的一个主要因素,而 SARS-CoV-2 的影响相对有限。hMPV 的显着临床影响表明其与该人群中 ARI 管理的相关性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查一系列呼吸道病毒在荷兰养老院居民中疑似急性呼吸道感染(ARI)的风险及其临床影响。
设计与对象
一项前瞻性队列研究研究于2023年3月至2025年5月期间进行,纳入了15家荷兰养老院中疑似患有急性呼吸道感染的居民。
核心方法
在第 0、14 和 28 天收集临床数据,并对残留的鼻咽和口咽拭子进行广泛的呼吸道病毒检测。包括季节性和 NH 水平随机效应的贝叶斯多级逻辑模型估计了疑似 ARI 风险,并按病毒比较了相关临床结果。
主要结果
3195名居民中,发生了526起疑似急性呼吸道感染事件。估计疑似 ARI 风险为 10.2%(95% 可信区间,1.7-43.8)。在 291 次发作中鉴定出病毒:严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2(SARS-CoV-2;29.0%)、甲型流感病毒(23.6%)、鼻病毒/肠道病毒(18.8%)、呼吸道合胞病毒(11.8%)、人类偏肺病毒(hMPV;6.4%)、副流感病毒(5.1%)和人类冠状病毒(5.4%)。
解释与创新
hMPV 的显着临床影响表明其与该人群中 ARI 管理的相关性,该研究得出的结论是,流感仍然是新罕布什尔州居民 ARI 负担的主要原因,而 SARS-CoV-2 的影响相对有限。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,疗养院居民需要有针对性的 监测 和流感和 hMPV 管理策略,特别是在疾病严重程度和恢复方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对残留拭子和仅摘要数据的依赖而受到限制,但由于前瞻性的 队列研究 设计和贝叶斯建模方法,证据的强度适中。

Clinical Impact of Acute Respiratory Virus Infections in Nursing Home Residents.

Authors: Lisa M. Kolodziej, Laura C Grootegoed, Menno D de Jong, Hertogh CMPM, Froukje Taat, Maureen Huisman, Rosanne Peuscher, Alvin X. Han, Laura W. van Buul, Sacha D. Kuil
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: To investigate the suspected acute respiratory infection (ARI) risk and the clinical impact of a range of respiratory viruses in Dutch nursing home (NH) residents. DESIGN: Prospective cohort study (March 2023-May 2025). SETTING AND PARTICIPANTS: Residents from 15 Dutch NHs were enrolled when suspected of an ARI. METHODS: Clinical data were collected on days 0, 14, and 28. Residual nasopharyngeal and oropharyngeal swabs were tested for a broad respiratory virus panel. A Bayesian multilevel logistic model including seasonal and NH-level random effects estimated suspected ARI risk. Relative clinical outcomes were compared by virus. RESULTS: Among 3195 residents, 526 suspected ARI episodes occurred. The estimated suspected ARI risk was 10.2% (95% credible interval, 1.7-43.8). Viruses were identified in 291 episodes: severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2; 29.0%), influenza A virus (23.6%), rhinovirus/enterovirus (18.8%), respiratory syncytial virus (11.8%), human metapneumovirus (hMPV; 6.4%), parainfluenza viruses (5.1%), and human coronaviruses (5.4%). hMPV and respiratory syncytial virus tended to be associated with higher odds of moderate or severe disease, and hMPV was linked to a 4-fold increase in bed hours. Influenza A was associated with delayed recovery, oxygen requirement, and a 3-fold risk of secondary bacterial pneumonia. SARS-CoV-2 cases had higher estimated odds of recovery by day 14 and spent fewer additional hours in bed during ARI episodes. CONCLUSIONS AND IMPLICATIONS: Influenza remains a major contributor to ARI burden in NH residents, whereas SARS-CoV-2 has relatively limited impact. The pronounced clinical impact of hMPV suggests its relevance for ARI management in this population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the suspected acute respiratory infection (ARI) risk and the clinical impact of a range of respiratory viruses in Dutch nursing home (NH) residents.
Design and population
A prospective cohort study was conducted from March 2023 to May 2025, enrolling residents from 15 Dutch nursing homes who were suspected of an acute respiratory infection.
Core methods
Clinical data were collected on days 0, 14, and 28, and residual nasopharyngeal and oropharyngeal swabs were tested for a broad respiratory virus panel. A Bayesian multilevel logistic model including seasonal and NH-level random effects estimated suspected ARI risk, and relative clinical outcomes were compared by virus.
Main results
Among 3195 residents, 526 suspected ARI episodes occurred. The estimated suspected ARI risk was 10.2% (95% credible interval, 1.7-43.8). Viruses were identified in 291 episodes: severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2; 29.0%), influenza A virus (23.6%), rhinovirus/enterovirus (18.8%), respiratory syncytial virus (11.8%), human metapneumovirus (hMPV; 6.4%), parainfluenza viruses (5.1%), and human coronaviruses (5.4%).
Interpretation and novelty
The pronounced clinical impact of hMPV suggests its relevance for ARI management in this population, and the study concludes that influenza remains a major contributor to ARI burden in NH residents, whereas SARS-CoV-2 has relatively limited impact.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for targeted surveillance and management strategies for influenza and hMPV in nursing home residents, particularly regarding disease severity and recovery.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on residual swabs and abstract-only data, but the evidence is moderate in strength due to the prospective cohort design and Bayesian modeling approach.

抗原 进化 的几何结构改善了流感 疫苗 选择

Geometry of antigenic evolution improves influenza vaccine selection
作者： Omid Arhami, Pejman Rohani
摘要中文翻译预测抗原 进化 对于选择有效的 季节性流感 A/H3N2 疫苗 菌株至关重要。为此,我们将 2002 年至 2025 年的血凝抑制和中和滴度整合到统一的贝叶斯抗原图谱中。该地图解析了十二个抗原簇,这些抗原簇以离散的步骤前进,其中几个簇在大多数季节共同循环。在 21 个季节中的 15 个季节中,WHO 推荐的 疫苗 属于比主要流行簇更早的簇。每次 疫苗 更新相对于最近病毒漂移的方向在样本外预测中预测了 疫苗 提前一个赛季的有效性。抗原距离(疫苗 病毒匹配的常规衡量标准)与有效性的相关性较弱,直到考虑到更新方向。按预测有效性对候选菌株进行回顾性排名,将选择一个预测在每个季节都优于世界卫生组织建议的菌株,将平均预测有效性提高 10 个百分点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
预测抗原 进化 对于选择有效的 季节性流感 A/H3N2 疫苗 菌株至关重要。该研究旨在通过将抗原数据整合到预测框架中来改进 疫苗 选择。
设计与对象
该研究将 2002 年至 2025 年的血凝抑制和中和滴度整合成统一的贝叶斯抗原图谱。这张图解析了十二个抗原簇以离散的步骤前进,其中几个簇在大多数季节共同循环。
核心方法
作者使用 2002 年至 2025 年的血凝抑制和中和滴度构建了贝叶斯抗原图谱。他们分析了 疫苗 相对于病毒漂移的更新方向,并根据预测的有效性对候选菌株进行了回顾性排名。
主要结果
在 21 个季节中的 15 个季节中,WHO 推荐的 疫苗 属于比主要流行簇更早的簇。在考虑更新方向之前,抗原距离与有效性的相关性较弱。
解释与创新
疫苗 更新方向相对于最近的病毒抗原漂移预测,可提前一个季节预测 疫苗 的有效性。如果回顾性地根据预测的有效性对毒株进行排序,将选择出在每个季节中表现优于世界卫生组织推荐毒株的毒株。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将 疫苗 更新方向纳入抗原距离测量中,可能提高 季节性流感 的有效性。这种方法可能为更准确的 疫苗 毒株选择策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于摘要级别的证据,这可能限制分析的深度。由于依赖历史数据且缺乏详细的统计验证,证据强度中等。

Geometry of antigenic evolution improves influenza vaccine selection

Authors: Omid Arhami, Pejman Rohani
Original abstract (English)
Anticipating antigenic evolution is essential for selecting effective seasonal influenza A/H3N2 vaccine strains. To this end, we integrated hemagglutination-inhibition and neutralization titers spanning 2002 to 2025 into a unified Bayesian antigenic map. The map resolves twelve antigenic clusters advancing in discrete steps, with several clusters co-circulating in most seasons. In 15 of 21 seasons, the WHO-recommended vaccine belonged to an earlier cluster than the dominant circulating cluster. The direction of each vaccine update relative to recent viral drift predicted vaccine effectiveness one season ahead in out-of-sample forecasts. Antigenic distance, the conventional measure of vaccine–virus match, was weakly associated with effectiveness until update direction was accounted for. Retrospectively ranking candidate strains by predicted effectiveness would have selected a strain predicted to outperform the WHO recommendation in every season, raising mean predicted effectiveness by 10 percentage points.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Anticipating antigenic evolution is essential for selecting effective seasonal influenza A/H3N2 vaccine strains. The study aimed to improve vaccine selection by integrating antigenic data into a predictive framework.
Design and population
The study integrated hemagglutination-inhibition and neutralization titers spanning 2002 to 2025 into a unified Bayesian antigenic map. This map resolved twelve antigenic clusters advancing in discrete steps, with several clusters co-circulating in most seasons.
Core methods
The authors constructed a Bayesian antigenic map using hemagglutination-inhibition and neutralization titers from 2002 to 2025. They analyzed the direction of vaccine updates relative to viral drift and retrospectively ranked candidate strains by predicted effectiveness.
Main results
In 15 of 21 seasons, the WHO-recommended vaccine belonged to an earlier cluster than the dominant circulating cluster. Antigenic distance was weakly associated with effectiveness until update direction was accounted for.
Interpretation and novelty
The direction of vaccine updates relative to recent viral drift predicted vaccine effectiveness one season ahead in out-of-sample forecasts. Retrospectively ranking strains by predicted effectiveness would have selected a strain outperforming the WHO recommendation in every season.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that incorporating the direction of vaccine updates into antigenic distance measurements could improve seasonal influenza vaccine effectiveness. This approach may inform more accurate vaccine strain selection strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, which may limit the depth of analysis. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on historical data and the absence of detailed statistical validation.

Journal: Clinical infectious diseases : an official publication of the Infectious Diseases Society of America  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-02  |  DOI: 10.1093/cid/ciaf579  |  Date basis: published_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 83, 2, 289-302
